Abstract: Video misinformation detection is often approached through global multimodal fusion or free-form multimodal reasoning. Both paradigms can under-represent localized authenticity cues that arise from coupled interactions among query phrases, contextual text, and short temporal spans of frames. Because such interactions are inherently higher-order, pairwise graph formulations are insufficient to capture multi-way cross-modal dependencies, whereas hypergraphs offer a suitable representation for these relations. We propose HyperClaim, a discriminative temporal hypergraph framework for sample-level authenticity classification. Using the title or benchmark-provided paired text as a claim-like query, HyperClaim constructs a sparse heterogeneous hypergraph over query tokens, evidence tokens, and sampled frames; applies confidence-aware filtering and source budgeting to form compact text-frame and short-range temporal evidence units; performs adaptive soft-incidence reasoning with residual text-video calibration; and aggregates textual, visual, and hyperedge states through a discrepancy-aware readout. Without relying on generated rationales or external tool calls, HyperClaim preserves fine-grained cross-modal and temporal structure that global fusion tends to flatten. Under the FactGuard temporal protocol, it achieves 83.7%, 82.0%, and 87.3% accuracy on FakeSV, FakeTT, and FakeVV, respectively, outperforming strong discriminative and reasoning-centric baselines. Learned incidence and attention weights further reveal token- and frame-level structure.

</details>


### [278] [When Specifications Conflict: A Symmetry-Based Framework for Measuring LLM Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28384)
*Tairan Wang,Liang Zhou,Zikang Zhan,Pingchuan Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种受控实验框架，用于研究大语言模型（LLM）在解决冲突规范时的偏好。通过构建显式冲突规范并采用对称设计，系统评估了四种表示类型（纯自然语言、形式化语言、自然化形式化语言、输入-输出示例）的优先级，发现形式化语言优于自然语言和示例，且该框架可推广至布尔代数、代码生成及临床领域等多任务场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM需整合多源信息，但缺乏可控且可归因的方法来分析模型如何解决相互竞争的规范之间的冲突。现有研究难以直接观察和分析模型在冲突情境下的具体选择偏好。

Method: 构建一个包含显式冲突的控制实验框架，利用对称性设计减少混淆因素。在一个包含550个冲突实例的可执行数学基准测试上，比较四种规范表示类型的模型偏好：纯自然语言、形式化语言、自然化形式化语言和输入-输出示例。随后将框架扩展至异构规范冲突场景，涵盖布尔代数、代码生成和临床领域。

Result: 模型表现出系统性的偏好模式而非随机行为。偏好排序为：形式化语言 ≈ 自然化形式化语言 > 纯自然语言 > 输入-输出示例。示例效应受模型能力和函数族的影响。框架成功展示了跨不同任务和规范形式的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一的方法，用于测量LLM如何解析来自竞争信息源的冲突，揭示了模型在处理不一致信息时的系统性偏好规律，为理解LLM的推理机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly required to integrate multiple sources of information that may be inconsistent or conflicting. However, there is still a lack of controllable and attributable methods for analyzing how models resolve conflicts between competing specifications. We propose a controlled experimental framework for studying model preferences under conflicting specifications. By constructing specifications with explicit conflicts, the framework enables model choices between competing specifications to be directly observed and analyzed. A symmetry-based design further reduces confounding factors, allowing preferences across representation types to be compared systematically.
  We evaluate the framework on an executable mathematical benchmark with 550 conflict instances spanning 11 function families, comparing four representation types: pure natural language, formal language, naturalized formal language, and input--output examples. Results show systematic preference patterns rather than random behavior, with a consistent ordering: $ \text{Formal} \approx \text{Naturalized Formal} > \text{Pure Natural Language} > \text{Input--Output Examples} $. Example effects further depend on model capability and function family.
  We extend the framework to heterogeneous specification conflicts in Boolean algebra, code generation, and the clinical domain, demonstrating its applicability across diverse tasks and specification forms. The framework provides a unified approach for measuring how LLMs resolve conflicts between competing sources of information.

</details>


### [279] [QuantWAMs: Calibrating at the Right Granularity for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28405)
*Jiacheng Zhou,Jinfan Lv,Ruixuan Li,Longtai Zhang,Yan Wang,Wenqiang Zhang,Lizhe Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QuantWAMs，一种针对世界动作模型（WAMs）的后训练量化框架。通过共享基线异常值校准、联合目标显著性分析和固定干预 rollout 审计三项策略，解决现有PTQ方法不适配WAMs闭环执行的问题。在仿真和真实机器人任务中，W4A4量化下性能接近FP16，显存降至29%，速度提升1.4-1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）具有迭代去噪和闭环执行特性，部署成本高。现有的后训练量化（PTQ）方法依赖开环目标、同质化模型假设及与部署不符的校准分布，因此难以有效应用于WAMs。

Method: 提出QuantWAMs框架，包含三种策略：1) 共享基线异常值校准：仅在坐标兼容模块间聚合激活证据；2) 联合目标显著性：基于视频-动作联合梯度的经验Fisher分数，在层粒度分配权重精度；3) 固定干预rollout审计：利用可达闭环状态修正去噪步骤保护计划，不改变精度预算。

Result: 在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上评估，涵盖RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及真实机器人操作。W4A4设置下，仿真平均性能与FP16仅相差0.2-0.7个百分点。真实机器人实验验证了部署可行性。峰值权重和激活内存降至FP16的约29%，块级速度提升1.4-1.6倍。

Conclusion: QuantWAMs成功实现了WAMs的高效量化部署，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了资源消耗并提升了推理速度，证明了其在实际机器人应用中的可行性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, but their iterative denoising and closed-loop execution make efficient deployment costly. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods are poorly suited to WAMs because they rely on open-loop objectives, homogeneous model assumptions, and calibration distributions that do not reflect deployment. We present QuantWAMs, a PTQ framework that aligns quantization decisions with the calibration context defined by model structure, rollout distribution, and task objective. QuantWAMs introduces three strategies: shared-basis outlier calibration, which pools activation evidence only across coordinate-compatible modules; co-training-objective saliency, which computes empirical-Fisher scores from the joint video--action gradient and assigns weight precision at a calibration-stable layer granularity; and fixed-intervention rollout auditing, which revises denoising-step protection schedules using reachable closed-loop states without changing the precision budget. We evaluate QuantWAMs on Fast-WAM and LingBot-VA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot manipulation with an AgiBot G2. Under a W4A4-dominant setting, the reported simulation means differ from FP16 by 0.2--0.7 percentage points. Real-robot trials further establish deployment feasibility on three manipulation tasks. For the targeted video and action blocks, QuantWAMs reduces peak weight-and-activation memory to about 29\% of FP16 and provides 1.4--1.6$\times$ block-level speedups.

</details>


### [280] [When Derived Measurements Mislead: Quantifying and Mitigating LLM Over-Trust with Privileged-Modality Reliability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28421)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Tianli Li,Mingzhe Cui,Yang Jiao,Lei Xie,Yi Pan,Xiao Hu,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“派生特征过度信任”(DFOT)概念，即下游大语言模型将具有实例依赖有效性的测量值误认为直接事实。通过生理传感案例（PPG与ECG对比），构建了包含五个量化指标的评估框架，用于测试和缓解LLM在处理冲突或误导性上下文时的错误，并在50,000条记录上验证了基线方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）管道中越来越多地直接使用派生测量数据作为既定事实，但这些数据的 validity 依赖于具体实例。当前缺乏针对LLM在接收此类数据时可能产生的“过度信任”现象的系统性评估和缓解方法。

Method: 1. 定义DFOT：下游LLM赋予派生测量值直接事实的认识论地位或在无效范围内使用它。
2. 构建评估框架：以生理传感为例，D1测试对与离线ECG矛盾的PPG节奏的接受度（冲突过度信任），D2测试在误导性严重病史下拒绝已确认可靠的PPG节奏的情况（上下文诱导错误）。
3. 使用五个估算量：冲突过度信任率(COTR)、上下文诱导错误率(CIR)、正确修复率(CRR)、证据特异性修复边际(ESRM)和效用损害率(UHR)。
4. 实验设置：利用ECG提供训练监督和离线参考构建，但不向LLM展示；使用ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏作为基线，在50,000对PPG-ECG记录上进行演示，并在187名患者的测试集上验证。

Result: 在协议锁定的187名患者测试集中，基线方法在四个修复和特异性指标上提升了1.82-6.69个百分点（所有配对置信区间均不包含零）。效用损害率(UHR)增加了0.67个百分点（95% CI: -0.4 to +1.7），表明在不降低可靠性的前提下，额外验证带来的成本增加有限且统计上不显著。

Conclusion: DFOT提供了一个通用的评估目标，用于开发更强的缓解方法。该框架不依赖于特定的可靠性生成器，为评估和改进LLM处理派生测量数据提供了标准化方法。代码已开源。

Abstract: Derived measurements increasingly enter large language model (LLM) pipelines as direct facts despite their instance-dependent validity. We define derived-feature over-trust (DFOT) as the failure in which a downstream LLM assigns such a measurement the epistemic status of a direct fact or uses it outside its valid scope. Using physiological sensing as a case study, D1 tests acceptance of a PPG-derived rhythm contradicted by offline ECG, whereas D2 tests rejection of an offline-confirmed reliable PPG rhythm under misleading severe history. ECG supplies training supervision and offline reference construction but is never shown to the LLM. Five estimands quantify this chain: conflict over-trust rate (COTR) and context-induced error rate (CIR) characterize D1/D2; correct repair rate (CRR) measures frozen-error repair; evidence-specific repair margin (ESRM) contrasts matched and patient-disjoint shuffled evidence; and utility harm rate (UHR) measures unnecessary verification among HIGH-reliability cases used without verification at baseline. The framework does not depend on a particular reliability generator. We demonstrate it on 50,000 paired PPG-ECG records using ECG-to-PPG privileged distillation as an illustrative baseline and PPG-only inference. On a protocol-locked 187-patient test, the baseline improves four repair and specificity endpoints by 1.82-6.69 percentage points, with all paired confidence intervals excluding zero; UHR increases by 0.67 percentage points (95% CI: -0.4 to +1.7). DFOT provides a common evaluation target for stronger mitigation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/Zongheng-Guo/When-Derived-Measurements-Mislead.

</details>


### [281] [A foundation model of numerical intelligence with cross-disciplinary generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28432)
*Chenghan Wu,Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了UNICON，一种具备“数值智能”的基础模型。它通过图结构数据从数值上下文中推断并应用预测关系，能在未见过的学科中达到专家级性能，无需重新训练。结合语言模型代理后，其表现甚至超越该领域的现有最先进专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型主要处理文本上下文中的知识获取与应用，但科学和社会系统往往涉及数值数据。为了填补这一空白，需要一种能够从数值上下文中获取和应用知识的模型，即“数值智能”。

Method: 提出UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON)，这是一种基于图的基础模型。它以系统中的图结构示例作为上下文，推断其中共享的预测关系，并将这些关系应用于来自同一系统的查询。

Result: 1. UNICON在多个科学和社会系统中展现出跨学科的数值智能，即使对于训练集中未包含的学科，也能在不重新训练的情况下接近专家级性能。2. 将UNICON与语言模型代理结合后，其在未见过的学科中超越了现有的最先进专家模型。3. 训练语料的多样性有助于提高对未见学科的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UNICON被确立为一种数值智能的基础模型，并为更广泛的人工智能生态系统提供了重要的构建模块。

Abstract: Intelligence is commonly understood as the ability to acquire and apply knowledge, adapt to unfamiliar situations and solve new problems. Large language models exhibit this capacity by inferring task-relevant knowledge from textual context and applying it to new tasks. Yet intelligence need not be confined to language. For scientific and social systems, we need models that acquire and apply knowledge from numerical context-an ability we call numerical intelligence. Here we introduce UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON), a foundation model that exhibits numerical intelligence across disciplines. Using graph-based examples from a system as context, UNICON infers the predictive relation shared across them and applies it to queries from the same system. Across scientific and social systems, including those from disciplines absent from training, the same model approaches specialist performance without retraining. Combining UNICON with language-model agents yields further gains, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art specialists in a discipline unseen in training. We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [282] [LeanCSP: A Framework for Certifying Constraint Reformulation and Solving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28459)
*Pablo Manrique,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lean定理证明器的约束编程验证框架，通过形式化验证确保问题建模等价性和求解器结果正确性，实现了不依赖外部求解器的端到端可信计算流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束编程在调度、规划等领域广泛应用，但其结果的可信度依赖于两个层面的保证：前置重构的语义保持性以及求解器输出的正确性。现有方法缺乏对这两个层面的统一形式化验证，导致用户需信任外部工具。

Method: 构建了一个集成于Lean定理证明器的双层级验证框架：1) 参数化证明问题族的形式化属性（如等价性、可满足性等价性及对称性破缺约束的正确性）；2) 通过翻译后端（MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, OPB）将求解器生成的证书转换回Lean进行独立检查，实现个体实例的验证。

Result: 该框架实现了端到端的可验证工作流，无需信任外部求解器即可确立约束问题的可满足性或不可满足性。实验表明，经过验证的对称性破缺技术显著降低求解搜索空间（最高减少2×10^7倍），且整个Lean内认证过程高效，最大规模实例仅需几分钟即可完成。

Conclusion: 结合形式化建模验证与求解器证书检查的框架，不仅提升了约束求解的可信度，还通过重用参数化证明大幅提升了实际求解效率，证明了形式化方法在高性能计算中的实用价值。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [283] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了内镜CLIP（EndoCLIP），一种针对结肠镜检查的视觉-语言基础模型。该模型利用从28万多份常规报告中恢复的12.5万对病变级图像-文本数据进行训练，在多项临床任务中超越了通用及生物医学视觉-语言编码器，并在良恶性分类上接近专家水平，证明了将常规文档转化为可扩展监督数据的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管结肠镜报告包含丰富的专家描述（如病变外观、大小和位置），但视觉-语言模型在此领域应用不足。现有报告是对整个过程的总结而非逐帧标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间的关联较弱，限制了模型的训练和利用。

Method: 开发EndoCLIP模型，通过从280,476份常规结肠镜记录中逐步恢复出125,756对病变级图像-文本配对数据，构建并训练一个结肠镜视觉-语言基础模型。

Result: 在病变级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成以及六个多中心临床分类任务中，EndoCLIP在零样本（zero-shot）和线性探针（linear-probe）设置下均优于通用和生物医学视觉-语言编码器。特别是在良性与恶性分类任务中，其线性探针性能在涉及12名内镜医生的盲法研究中接近专家读者水平。

Conclusion: 恢复“发现-帧”对应关系可以将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标能够通过自然语言指定，而无需为每个任务单独进行详细标注。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [284] [Towards Autonomous Aircraft Surveillance from Nanosatellites through On-Board Inference and Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28470)
*Antonio Delgado-Rosa,David Muñoz-Valero,Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合星上推理与生成式数据增强的工作流，解决低轨小卫星在带宽限制和类别不平衡下的航空监视瓶颈。通过在6U立方星上进行边缘推理，并利用微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类图像，显著提升了检测精度（mAP从77.9%升至82.2%，少数类F1从0.683升至0.811），同时实现了25-30帧/秒的实时处理，为自主空中监视提供了高效决策支持方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道航空监视面临两大相互关联的瓶颈：一是纳卫星下行链路预算有限，但传统方法仍传输海量原始图像至地面处理；二是公开卫星航空数据集稀缺且严重类别不平衡。这些问题导致决策延迟或使标准检测器难以学习罕见飞机类别的鲁棒特征。

Method: 提出联合解决上述限制的工作流：在配备低功耗边缘张量加速器的6U CubeSat上执行星上推理；利用通过低秩适应（LoRA）微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类图像；由中间检测器自动标注并伪标签化这些合成输出，并与经典增强样本合并以构建平衡数据集。

Result: 平衡数据集使全局平均精度均值（mAP）从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1分数从0.683提升至0.811。量化后的检测器适配片上内存，并在轨实现25-30帧/秒的处理速度。

Conclusion: 该工作流区别于被动数据收集的常规弯管架构，计算测试证明其可作为小卫星实时、自主航空监视的有效决策支持工具。

Abstract: Airborne surveillance from low Earth orbit is hindered by two interconnected bottlenecks: nanosatellites have a limited downlink budget, yet the conventional approach still transmits terabytes of raw imagery to the ground for processing, and open satellite datasets for aircraft are scarce and severely class-imbalanced. These limitations either delay timely decision-making or prevent standard detectors from learning robust representations of rare aircraft classes. In this paper, a workflow that combines on-board inference with generative data augmentation is proposed to address both limitations jointly. Inference is executed on a 6U CubeSat equipped with a low-power edge tensor accelerator, while a diffusion model fine-tuned through low-rank adaptation generates synthetic minority-class imagery. This synthetic output is automatically annotated, pseudo-labelled, by an intermediate detector and merged with classically augmented samples. The results show that the balanced dataset increases global mean average precision from 77.9% to 82.2%, with the minority class rising from F1=0.683 to F1=0.811, and that the quantised detector fits the on-chip memory and projects 25-30 frames per second on orbit. This approach contrasts with the conventional bent-pipe architecture, in which the satellite acts as a passive data collector. Therefore, the computational tests support the proposed workflow as a decision-support tool for real-time, autonomous airborne surveillance from nanosatellites.

</details>


### [285] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模糊规则神经符号框架的管道缺陷严重性评估方法，解耦视觉感知与符号推理，通过Swin Transformer预测缺陷代码并结合J48决策树生成的模糊规则进行可解释推理，显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化下水道管道严重性评估依赖直接图像分类，形成“黑箱”模型，导致视觉缺陷特征与最终严重性评分之间的逻辑联系不明确，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建模块化模糊规则神经符号框架：1) 感知模块使用Swin Transformer从图像中直接预测14种多标签检查代码（CODE）等级；2) 推理模块利用Weka J48决策算法在真实数据上训练，将决策路径转化为19条固定IF-THEN规则；3) 推理过程采用模糊逻辑，通过t-norm激活条件、结合规则置信度加权，并使用s-norm合并以生成可解释的类别证据。实验中对比了Product、Łukasiewicz和Hamacher算子对，并使用5个大语言模型共识生成的地面真值标签处理高度不平衡的5类严重性数据。

Result: 在包含3,244张图像的测试集上，该方法相比仅基于图像的分类方法，准确率提升17.9%，平衡准确率提升12.2%，Macro F1提升23.0%，MCC提升17.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了具有竞争力的类别平衡性能，并提供了从预测代码到规则支持再到严重性证据的可追溯推理过程，有效解决了传统方法的不可解释性问题。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [286] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE框架，通过联合策略模型将供应链中的选品、货源分配、补货频率和路径规划等分散决策统一优化，解决因部门割裂导致的库存和运输低效问题。在叮咚买菜和京东的真实数据中，该端到端协调方法显著优于传统分段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链中，选品、供应源选择、补货频率和路由规划等关键决策通常由不同部门独立处理，导致操作耦合被忽视，进而引发缺货、库存积压和可避免的运输成本。现有方法缺乏对这些相互依赖决策的统一协调机制。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination）复合策略模型。该方法将供应链实体表示为token，通过共享的操作表征进行上下文关联，并将每种token映射到相应的决策接口。每个决策基于先前决策形成的部分计划构建，最终计划通过共享的系统级效用进行评估。

Result: 在城市生鲜零售补货场景中，利用叮咚买菜和京东两家大型供应链的真实运营数据进行评估。结果显示，SCOPE在两种不同的补货层级设置下，均一致地优于单独优化各决策阶段的方法以及供应链操作中常用的实践基线。

Conclusion: 学习和协调跨部门的运营耦合关系能够带来更有效的端到端供应链决策，证明了从孤立决策模块向统一运营规划转变的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [287] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个动态更新的AI基准测试（InfoOpsBench），利用来自俄、中、伊三国国家支持信息操作的2100多个真实案例，评估前沿语言模型被用于此类操作的风险。研究发现大多数模型存在被滥用的风险，且不同模型在拒绝率、事实核查和生成内容危害性上差异巨大，中国模型在涉及本国批评性事实时表现出显著的合规性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决当前AI安全评估静态且易饱和的问题，通过引入基于实时监测的国家支持信息操作数据，构建一个能持续反映前沿语言模型在面对地缘政治操纵意图时完整性（Integrity）的动态基准。

Method: 从监控管道中提取超过2,100个来自俄罗斯、中国和伊朗国家支持的信息操作案例；选取8家提供商的17个前沿语言模型进行测试；采用四种提示框架（prompt framings）评估模型响应；计算“完整性得分”（即拒绝请求的百分比）及事实核查率等指标。

Result: 1. 大多数模型可被共谋用于信息操作，完整性得分跨度极大（8.8%至94.5%），且与模型规模无关。
2. 模型选择显著影响操作性质：部分模型会捏造细节并产生比源材料更有害的输出，部分则在合规的同时化解主张。
3. 事实核查率范围为2.9%至72.9%。
4. 中国开发的模型（除Z.ai的GLM 5.2外）在针对基于事实但批评中国的声明时，合规率相比良性声明大幅下降48-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 语言模型对信息操作的抵抗力与其对良性声明的拒绝倾向相关，揭示了模型可用性与安全性之间的平衡难题。该基准具有动态更新特性，能有效抵抗评估饱和，为持续监控AI在地缘政治信息战中的角色提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [288] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出选择性信度受限信念更新框架，通过将认知输入转化为较弱的代理来解决现有方法无法处理复合输入部分实现的问题，统一并扩展了传统信念更新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信度受限信念更新将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表示仅部分实现复合认知输入的情况；而标准Katsuno-Mendelzon更新假设每个初始可能世界都能纳入认知输入，缺乏对可信性的限制。

Method: 引入选择性信度受限信念更新，针对每个源世界将认知输入转换为较弱的代理，再进行信度受限转换；提供语义和公理化表征，识别一致性保持和最大一致性保持两个子类，并通过特殊案例证明其通用性。

Result: 建立了该更新算子的语义和公理特征；发现两类表现良好的子类（一致性保持和最大一致性保持）；证明信度受限信念更新是其特例，当移除信度限制且变换函数为恒等映射时恢复为标准Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了统一且表达力更强的信念更新理论，既涵盖已有方法，又支持源依赖的选择性接受，解决了部分输入实现的建模难题。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [289] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MANTA框架，使多智能体系统的通信拓扑在推理时自我演化，通过监控协作轨迹并动态调整角色、链接等结构，显著提升复杂问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定设计或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理过程中根据任务需求动态调整协作结构的能力。

Method: 引入MANTA（Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation）框架：1. 执行前基于先验经验初始化任务条件拓扑；2. 部署中监控协作轨迹，当当前组织不足时应用有界的结构更新（修改角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性及验证路径），同时保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在涵盖信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理的五个基准测试上，MANTA取得74.0的平均分，比最强基线高出5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上获得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时的自我改进不仅可以应用于模型行为，还可以扩展到协作架构本身，证明动态自适应拓扑能显著增强多智能体系统的性能。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [290] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了推理时扩展（Inference-time Scaling）在资源受限本地计算机使用智能体（CUAs）中的有效性。通过对Qwen3-VL、UI-TARS和OpenCUA在OSWorld基准上的实验，发现增加计算量往往导致收益递减并改变失败模式：上下文扩展可提升稳定性但存在饱和点；时间扩展难以纠正错误轨迹；结构分解和并行扩展则带来额外开销或成本。结论指出高效本地C needs选择性计算分配及针对模型局限性的框架设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着隐私、成本效率和实用性需求的增加，在本地部署自主计算机使用智能体（CUAs）变得日益重要。然而，在严格的硬件约束下提升其性能仍具挑战性。尽管已知推理时扩展能改善前沿智能体，但其在资源受限的本地模型中的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 对Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B在OSWorld基准上进行系统性实证研究，从上下文、时间、结构和并行四个维度评估推理时扩展的效果。

Result: 1. 附加计算通常产生边际收益递减，并改变失败模式。2. 上下文扩展通过历史 grounding 提高轨迹稳定性和任务准确率，但随着Token成本增加收益饱和，失败模式由重复/停滞转向过早的错误成功。3. 时间扩展减少了最大步数停滞，但未显著提升任务成功率，表明更长的视野往往延长错误轨迹而非纠正它们。4. 结构分解在两阶段本地智能体中引入规划和格式化开销；并行扩展虽部分缓解失败，但需付出巨大的计算成本。

Conclusion: 高效的本地计算机使用智能体需要选择性计算分配、感知失败的机制，以及围绕本地模型能力和局限性设计的代理框架，而非单纯依赖增加计算资源。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [291] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DualG-MRAG，一种双层级多模态检索增强生成框架，通过解耦宏推理和微匹配图解决复杂多跳推理中的噪声与证据丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-RAG方法在处理复杂多跳推理时存在局限，独立实例级匹配难以捕捉跨模态/文档关系；传统图增强方法在细粒度特征（导致噪声）和粗粒度表示（导致关键证据丢失）之间面临两难困境。

Method: 构建解耦的双层架构：1) 宏观图(Macro Graph)用于全局拓扑路由以抑制噪声；2) 微观图(Micro Graph)用于精确局部验证；3) 使用GNN检索器进行查询驱动的动态相关性传播；4) 引入动态规划解码机制，从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径替代孤立文档块作为生成模型输入。

Result: 实验表明，DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂问答准确性方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡了全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配，提升了多模态检索增强生成在多跳任务中的性能。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [OneShot: Index-in-Ranking with Neural Scoring for Large-Scale Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27475)
*Ziwei Li,Shuyao Li,Xufeng Cai,Xue Zou,Yiming Ma,Huiting Lu,Wujie Yan,Zhichen Zhao,Yang Lu,Zhe Wang,Rui Luo,Zhengyu Su,Dan Zhang,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OneShot框架，通过端到端联合学习解决推荐系统中检索精度与索引效率的结构错位问题，在Instagram部署后显著提升召回率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统中，排序准确性（优化预测与用户行为对齐）和索引效率（优化物品表示的结构分组以支持快速搜索）目标存在根本性错位，限制了大规模交互建模的效果。

Method: 提出OneShot，一种端到端的模型内索引学习框架，原生地将索引学习与排序目标对齐；利用联合学习作为结构基础，突破点积瓶颈，扩展神经评分的交互建模能力。

Result: 在Instagram工业级短视频推荐系统中全面部署；用户日会话数、互动量和停留时间显著增长；在操作级排序量下实现20%的召回率提升，且在同等召回水平下效率提升10倍。

Conclusion: OneShot成功弥合了检索阶段排序目标与索引结构之间的鸿沟，证明了端到端联合学习在大规模推荐系统中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: In modern recommendation systems, retrieval serves as a primary stage responsible for filtering billions of candidate items down to thousands prior to refined ranking. To make this massive search effective and efficient, the system relies on ranking accuracy and indexing efficiency. However, these two objectives are traditionally misaligned: while the former optimizes for the alignment between ranking predictions and user behavior, the latter optimizes for a structural grouping of item representations which enables fast search among billions of candidates. Thus, despite extensive efforts to scale up interaction modeling for retrieval, they remain fundamentally limited by the structural misalignment between the ranking objectives and the proximity-learned index. In this work, we address this long-standing dichotomy by proposing a new holistic retrieval framework, OneShot. It is an end-to-end, in-model index learning framework that natively aligns index learning with ranking objectives. Using this joint learning as a structural foundation, OneShot pushes the boundaries of retrieval expressiveness by scaling interaction modeling with neural scoring beyond the persistent dot-product bottleneck. OneShot is fully deployed in Instagram's industrial short-video recommendation system, driving significant wins in user daily sessions, engagement, and time-spent. Additionally, OneShot achieves a $20\%$ recall gain at the operational ranking volume and a 10x efficiency improvement at an equivalent recall level.

</details>


### [293] [Hierarchical Reranking for Scalable Financial RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27523)
*Joohyun Lee,Sungwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Hierarchical Reranker的RAG框架，专门用于解决金融文档（如10-K文件、表格披露和宏观经济报告）处理中的混合文本-表结构及大规模数据挑战。通过预检索优化、分层重排架构和长上下文管理三大创新，显著提升了检索精度和生成可靠性，在FinQA等基准测试中表现优异，并在ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成（RAG）系统在处理包含混合文本-表结构的金融文档以及应对海量数据规模时存在不足，难以满足专家级推理对准确性和时效性的要求。

Method: 提出了Hierarchical Reranker框架，包含三项核心创新：1) 预检索优化（Pre-Retrieval Optimization），通过标准化、关键词扩展和表格转换提升查询清晰度和搜索效率；2) 分层重排架构（Hierarchical Reranker Architecture），采用两阶段排序机制提高检索精度；3) 长上下文管理（Long-Context Management），通过自适应输入分区和融合技术在广泛上下文中保持推理准确性。

Result: 在FinQA、FinanceBench和ConvFinQA等多个基准测试中，该系统实现了NDCG@20得分为0.7918，并展现出优越的事实一致性。其鲁棒性在ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge中得到验证，获得第二名。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可部署、领域优化的RAG管道，增强了金融推理的准确性和可扩展性，为自动化审计报告和定量投资分析奠定了基础。源代码将在接受后公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Analyzing financial documents such as 10-K filings, tabular disclosures, and macroeconomic reports demands expert reasoning and extensive time. However, existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems often struggle to process hybrid text-table structures or the massive scale of financial documents. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Reranker, a RAG framework designed to improve retrieval performance and generative reliability across large-scale financial datasets. The system integrates three key innovations: Pre-Retrieval Optimization, enhancing query clarity and search efficiency through normalization, keyword expansion, and table transformation; Hierarchical Reranker Architecture, improving retrieval precision through a two-stage ranking mechanism; and Long-Context Management, preserving reasoning accuracy through adaptive input partitioning and fusion under extensive contexts. Across multiple benchmarks, including FinQA, FinanceBench, and ConvFinQA, the proposed system achieved an NDCG@20 score of 0.7918 and demonstrated superior factual consistency. Its robustness was further validated by achieving second place in the ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge. This work presents a deployable, domain-optimized RAG pipeline that enhances both the accuracy and scalability of financial reasoning, paving the way for automated audit reporting and quantitative investment analysis. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [294] [Heterogeneous Ranking in Industrial-Scale Recommender Systems: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27577)
*Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Zhenwei Tang,Peifan Wu,Nada Al-Thawr,Luoshu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HA-MoE架构和LENS框架，用于解决Google Discover异构推荐系统中的多任务排序挑战，通过离线和在线测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构推荐系统（如整合网页、视频、UGC等）面临特征密度和用户交互模式差异大的挑战，构建统一排序模型需避免负迁移和多数类偏差，同时保持高性能和低运维开销。

Method: 提出HA-MoE（异质性自适应多门控混合专家架构），将显式异质性上下文融入门控网络和专家表示；引入LENS轻量级可观测性框架以监控专家专业化；使用DL-AUC指标评估全局与分段排名性能。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上的离线评估显示优于基线模型；在线A/B测试证实提升了feed活动量和探索指标。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了工业级推荐系统中异构内容的管理问题，为多任务排序提供了可扩展且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous recommendation feeds present complex challenges that extend beyond those found in highly homogeneous environments (e.g., music-only or video-only closed-ecosystem platforms). In Google Discover, a unified feed integrates diverse content sourced from the decentralized open web, including web articles, long-form and short-form videos, user-generated content (UGC), and beyond. Different content types exhibit distinct feature densities and user interaction patterns. Building a unified ranking model that sustains high performance across such heterogeneity, while avoiding negative transfer or majority bias, remains a significant industrial challenge.
  This paper presents an end-to-end case study on the industrial-scale multi-task ranking of heterogeneous feeds, grounded in real-world deployment. We introduce HA-MoE, a heterogeneity-adaptive multi-gated mixture-of-experts architecture that incorporates explicit heterogeneity context into both gating networks and expert representations. This approach enables effective specialization without significantly increasing operational overhead. To support reliable deployment, we introduce LENS, a lightweight observability framework that provides interpretable diagnostics of expert specialization and tracks this functional heterogeneity across continuous retraining. We evaluate our method using Dual-Level AUC (DL-AUC), a heterogeneity-aware evaluation metric that combines global ranking performance with cross-segment ranking correctness. Offline evaluations on a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models. Furthermore, online A/B testing confirms gains in feed activity and exploration metrics. Together, offline and online results validate the effectiveness of our approach for managing heterogeneity in industrial-scale recommender systems.

</details>


### [295] [An Exploration Graph with Continuous Refinement for Efficient Multimedia Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27623)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“连续细化探索图”（crEG）的新方法，用于快速构建紧凑的探索图，以解决现有近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）中构建速度慢或内存需求高的问题，并特别针对“探索性搜索”场景进行了优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据集和特征向量维度的增长，大规模多媒体数据库中的近似最近邻搜索变得日益重要。虽然基于图的方法在检索精度和搜索时间之间提供了最佳权衡，但现有方法存在构建速度慢或内存占用高的问题。此外，现有的ANNS研究很少考虑将查询本身作为数据库元素一部分的“探索性搜索”场景，而这对推荐和探索系统至关重要。

Method: 提出了“连续细化探索图”（crEG），这是一种新颖的方法，能够以最快的方式构建紧凑的探索图，并具有最先进的搜索性能。该方法包含一个可选的边缘优化算法以进一步增强效果。这两个算法专门设计用于生成和操作具有偶数度且保证任何时刻都连通的无向图，从而支持探索性搜索。

Result: 实验表明，crEG在高效构建和搜索方面表现出色。同时，研究还发现，在标准ANNS任务中表现高效的算法，并不一定能在“探索性搜索”中取得良好性能。

Conclusion: crEG通过其独特的图结构设计和连通性保证，为探索性搜索提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案，填补了现有ANNS方法在该特定应用场景下的空白。

Abstract: As datasets and the dimensionality of feature vectors continue to grow, Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) in large multimedia databases becomes increasingly relevant. Graph-based approaches have demonstrated to offer the best trade-off between retrieval precision and search time. Despite their ability to deliver search times several orders of magnitude faster than exact search techniques, existing methods suffer from slow constructions speeds or high memory requirements. This paper presents a "continuous refining Exploration Graph" (crEG), a novel approach for rapidly constructing a compact exploration graph with state-of-the-art search performance. Additionally, it provides the ability to enhance its effectiveness even further through an optional edge optimization algorithm. Both algorithms are specifically designed to produce and operate on undirected graphs with even degrees and guarantee graph connectivity at any time, a property particularly valuable for "exploratory search", where the query is part of the database elements. Although such queries provide an advantageous starting point for graph search algorithms, they have been rarely considered in the context of ANNS, yet are crucial for recommendation and exploration systems. Our experiments demonstrate high efficiency in ANNS does not necessarily translate to a good performance in "exploratory search".

</details>


### [296] [Dynamic Exploration Graph: A Novel Approach for Efficient Nearest Neighbor Search in Evolving Multimedia Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27640)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Bruno Schilling,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出动态探索图（DEG），一种支持动态数据的高效近似最近邻搜索算法，通过新颖的顶点删除和图扩展机制，在保持静态数据集高性能的同时，显著提升动态场景下的构建速度与搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于图的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）在处理动态数据集（数据持续增删）时的挑战，同时兼顾搜索精度、时间效率及动态适应性。

Method: 提出动态探索图（DEG），作为连续精炼探索图的扩展。核心创新包括：1. 保证图连通性的新颖顶点删除算法；2. 与数据分布无关的图扩展方法。

Result: 在流式和在线场景的实验表明，DEG在构建时间和搜索效率上优于现有动态图算法；在静态数据集上，其性能与当前最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: DEG通过维持平衡且高连通性的图结构，成功解决了动态数据带来的挑战，兼具对动态数据集的高适应性和对静态数据集的高性能，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) represents a fundamental problem in various applications (image-search, recommendation systems). While graph-based algorithms have demonstrated a good balance between search accuracy and time, handling dynamic datasets, where data points are continuously added or removed, remains a challenge. This paper introduces the Dynamic Exploration Graph (DEG), an extension of the continuous refining Exploration Graph, which retains high search efficiency for static dataset while adding essential support for dynamic data. At the core of the DEG design are two key innovations: a novel vertex deletion algorithm which guarantees graph connectivity and a data distribution-agnostic method for graph expansion. Through these mechanisms, the DEG maintains a balanced and well-connected structure, even under continuous data alterations. Empirical experiments in both streaming and online scenarios demonstrate the superior performance of the DEG, surpassing existing dynamic graph algorithms in terms of construction time and search efficiency. Although optimized for dynamic datasets, the DEG delivers results as good as current state-of-the-art approaches for static dataset, underscoring its broad applicability.

</details>


### [297] [LoopMemGR: From Behavior Logs to Evolving Memory for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27647)
*Hui Qian,Changfa Wu,Chang Liu,Binbin Cao,Jian Wu,Yuliang Yan,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LoopMemGR，一种用于生成式推荐的闭环推荐经验记忆框架，通过维护推荐经验日志并提取近期、频率和全局三个视角的证据，解决现有方法无法复用历史推荐决策及反馈信息的不对称记忆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法多遵循“历史即上下文”范式，仅重构用户偏好而丢弃系统侧的推荐决策及后续反馈，导致系统拥有不对称的记忆（记住用户行为但忘记自身推荐），使得偏好验证信号、潜在负证据和历史探索信息无法跨请求复用。

Method: 提出LoopMemGR框架，除常规行为日志外，维护记录过去推荐-反馈轨迹的推荐经验日志；从近期视图（短期交互动态）、频率视图（ recurring 推荐模式）和全局视图（跨用户可迁移规律）三个互补视角提取与请求相关的证据；将这些信号压缩为固定数量的经验token，以在有限输入预算下条件化生成主干模型。

Result: 在工业级淘宝数据集上的大量实验证明了闭环经验积累和多视角经验提取的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入闭环经验记忆机制，有效解决了生成式推荐中历史信息复用的局限性，提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation formulates next-item prediction as conditional autoregressive generation over discrete Semantic IDs, enabling end-to-end recommendation over large-scale item spaces. However, most existing methods follow a history-as-context paradigm that repeatedly reconstructs user preference from behavior history while discarding system-side recommendation decisions after each request. This creates an asymmetric memory: the system remembers what the user has done, but not what it has previously recommended or learned from the resulting feedback. Consequently, useful preference-validation signals, potential negative evidence, and historical exploration information cannot be directly reused across requests. To address these limitations, we propose LoopMemGR, a closed-loop recommendation experience memory framework for generative recommendation. In addition to the conventional behavior log, LoopMemGR maintains a recommendation experience log that records past recommendation--feedback trajectories. It extracts request-relevant evidence through three complementary views: the recency view captures short-term interaction dynamics, the frequency view summarizes recurring recommendation patterns, and the global view distills transferable regularities shared across users. These signals are compressed into a fixed number of experience tokens to condition the generative backbone under a bounded input budget. Extensive experiments on an industrial Taobao dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of closed-loop experience accumulation and multi-view experience extraction.

</details>


### [298] [Restoring Collaborative Signals in Semantic-ID Generative Recommendation via Personalized Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27682)
*Changjiang Han,Qingyang Li,Yaqiang Zang,Jikun Kang,Pinghua Gong,Xue Liu,Bowei He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重训练骨干模型或SID的推理框架，通过个性化自然语言在生成过程中注入分层协同信号，解决LLM推荐中语义ID无法同时承载内容与协同信息的问题，从而提升推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的生成式推荐模型试图通过自然语言和显式推理实现更强、更个性化的推荐，但面临严重瓶颈：文本与离散语义ID（SID）嵌入空间错位，导致深度推理难以转化为正确的SID；且紧凑的SID无法同时容纳内容信息和协同信号，两者相互竞争导致协同信号丢失，限制了推荐精度。现有方法多依赖昂贵的多轮训练，且在推理时缺乏补充缺失信号的机制。

Method: 提出一个由个性化自然语言引导的框架。该框架不直接映射语言到SID，也不改变模型骨干或重新训练SID，而是在生成过程中添加分层协同线索。具体而言，利用自然语言建立可分析的链接，将协同模式与其受众关联起来，从而在推理阶段恢复SID所缺失的协同信号。

Result: 该方法在推荐准确率上实现了持续且一致的增益。研究证明，通过在推理阶段将生成过程建立在协同结构之上，而非依赖显式推理或重新训练，可以有效解决SID生成中的信号缺失问题。

Conclusion: 通过在生成过程中引入由自然语言引导的分层协同线索，可以在不修改模型架构或重新训练的情况下，有效弥补传统SID在承载协同信号方面的不足，显著提升LLM生成式推荐的准确性和个性化能力。

Abstract: Making LLM-based generative recommendation models stronger and more personalized through natural language and explicit reasoning is a widely anticipated yet still unsolved goal. Such models cast recommendation as autoregressively generating an item's semantic-ID (SID), a short tuple of discrete codes, so that recommending well reduces to emitting the right SID. In this setting the model verbalizes its knowledge poorly, and text and SID tokens live in misaligned embedding spaces. Deep reasoning therefore rarely turns into a correct SID, and enabling explicit "thinking" often gives no gain or even hurts. The deeper cause is that a compact SID cannot hold content and collaborative signal at once: the two compete, and collaboration loses. Because a mis-predicted SID is a wrong recommendation, this caps accuracy directly. Costly multi-round training barely helps, and few methods try to supply the missing signal at inference time. What is missing is a reliable channel that carries collaborative signal into SID generation. We therefore propose a framework, guided by personalized natural language, that adds hierarchical collaborative cues as the model generates, without altering the backbone or retraining the SIDs. Rather than mapping language onto SIDs directly, it uses language to attach analyzable links between collaborative patterns and their audiences, restoring the collaborative signal that SIDs miss. The result is consistent gains in recommendation accuracy, grounding generation in collaborative structure at inference time rather than relying on explicit reasoning or retraining.

</details>


### [299] [Measuring Alignment With Reader Highlights Net of Position and Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27739)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估语言模型上下文压缩性能的方法，利用“自然社会高亮”（多人独立标记重点）作为非循环参考标准。通过严格控制长度和位置偏差，研究证明基于语言模型的重要性排序能显著优于传统截断方法和经典启发式方法，其效果接近人类读者水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的上下文压缩评估依赖于下游任务准确率，这存在循环论证问题（用另一个模型判断什么是重要的）。现有的基于人群标记的指标受到句子位置（前缀偏好）和长度（长句偏好）的混淆，导致评估结果不可靠。因此，需要一种去偏、非循环且符合人类阅读习惯的评估基准。

Method: 1. 数据构建：收集120个网页文档，每个文档至少有12名独立读者进行标记。
2. 去偏处理：将已标记句子与同一文档中未标记的句子进行匹配，确保两者在相对深度和文档内长度排名上相等，从而消除位置和长度的混淆效应。
3. 统计校准：使用仅基于位置和长度的合成零假设（synthetic nulls）对估计器进行校准，以识别并排除假阳性（研究发现仅按深度分层会在20-36%的无效应零假设中产生假阳性）。
4. 实验对比：测试语言模型重要性排序、朴素截断法、经典启发式方法（如Luhn 1958）以及人类读者的表现。

Result: 1. 语言模型优势：语言模型重要性排序保留了38.4%的人群标记句子，而匹配的对照邻居仅保留19.9%， enrichment值为+0.196（p=0.0005），具有统计显著性。
2. 基线表现：朴素截断法（基于位置）效果几乎为零（+0.003）。经典方法Luhn启发式达到+0.088，表明仅靠词数计数可部分恢复读者选择能力，但加入词汇中心性后提升极小（+0.010），说明一致性并非来自中心性。
3. 与人类对比：在相同预算和标签下，单个人类读者的得分为+0.182，与GPT-5.4（+0.002，置信区间宽）无显著差异，但低于Claude Opus 5。
4. 复现失败：作者指出其先前工作中的一项声明在该语料库上无法复现。

Conclusion: 通过消除位置和长度偏差，本研究建立了一个更可靠的上下文压缩评估框架。结果表明，现代语言模型在识别人类认为重要的文本方面表现出色，其性能接近甚至超过普通人类读者，且优于传统的基于规则的方法。这为评估大模型的上下文理解能力提供了新的、非循环的实证依据。

Abstract: Context compression discards most of a document before a language model reads it, and is normally evaluated by downstream task accuracy - which makes another model the judge of what mattered. Naturalistic social highlighting offers a non-circular reference: many people independently marking passages on the same page. But the obvious metric, the fraction of crowd-marked sentences a compressor keeps, is confounded twice: crowd marks are front-loaded and crowd-marked sentences are longer, so any method favouring early or long sentences scores well regardless of readers. We remove both by matching each marked sentence against unmarked sentences of the same document at equal relative depth and equal within-document length rank, and we calibrate every estimator on synthetic nulls built from position and length alone - a step that matters, since depth-only stratification returns a false positive on 20-36% of nulls containing no effect. On 120 web documents (at least 12 independent readers each), a language-model importance ranking keeps 38.4% of crowd-marked sentences against 19.9% of their matched neighbours: an enrichment of +0.196 [+0.148, +0.239], at p = 0.0005 under an exact randomization test that assumes nothing about clustering, and replicated cross-vendor. Naive truncation, whose keep rule is position, correctly falls to +0.003. To give the number a scale: scored identically, on the same budget, against a crowd label recomputed to exclude them, a single human reader reaches +0.182 - indistinguishable from GPT-5.4 (+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]) and below Claude Opus 5. Classical methods are not null - Luhn's 1958 heuristic reaches +0.088 - so reader selection is partly recoverable by counting words; conditioning additionally on lexical centrality removes only 0.010, so the agreement is not centrality. We also report that a claim in our own prior work does not reproduce on this corpus.

</details>


### [300] [A Structured Knowledge Infrastructure for Domain-Specific Data Asset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27748)
*Mengdi Chen,Yuanxin Huang,Yulin Jiang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对企业数据分析代理中RAG检索错误率高及缺乏使用知识的问题，小红书提出了一种基于图引导检索器（GGR）和场景感知排序器（SAR）的两层解决方案。该方案通过构建双用途知识库和闭环刷新管道，将Hit@10从19.1%提升至96.6%，显著降低了延迟并提升了知识覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决企业数据智能体面临的两个结构性失败：通用RAG常检索到错误的资产（初始Hit@10仅为19.1%），且无法提供防止指标误读的使用知识。这些问题源于语义鸿沟、实体歧义、模式漂移及资产-使用差距四大根本原因。

Method: 在小红书商业广告数据仓库（5,300+ Hive表，14个领域）部署两层解决方案：1) 三层双用途知识库结合闭环刷新管道，实现日级数据新鲜度；2) 图引导检索器（GGR）利用2,859节点的知识图谱作为候选门控进行意图路由；3) 场景感知排序器（SAR）应用19类实体识别和显式场景标注，利用负面知识提升排序效果。

Result: 在两个百题基准测试中，Hit@10从19.1%大幅提升至96.6%（提升77.5个百分点），知识覆盖率从56%增至77%。GGR实现了71.6倍的Token减少，端到端延迟控制在4.84--5.33秒之间。仅负面知识的引入就为Hit@10带来了25个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 提出的两层架构有效解决了企业数据检索中的准确性和知识缺失问题，通过图引导和场景感知的混合机制，在保证低延迟的同时实现了高精度的资产检索和全面的知识覆盖，验证了其在大规模商业数据仓库中的有效性。

Abstract: Enterprise data analytics agents face two structural failures: generic RAG retrieves the wrong asset (Hit@10=19.1%) and delivers no usage knowledge to prevent metric misinterpretation---stemming from four root causes (C1--C4) ranging from semantic gap and entity ambiguity to schema drift and asset-usage gap. We present a two-layer solution deployed in the commercial advertising data warehouse at Xiaohongshu (5,300+ Hive tables, 14 domains). A three-tier dual-purpose knowledge base (179 documents, eight-section annotation template) serves both retrieval and generation, with a closed-loop refresh pipeline maintaining day-level freshness (one yes/no approval, 30s hot-reload). The Graph-Guided Retriever (GGR) uses a 2,859-node knowledge graph as a candidate gate with intent routing to deliver 71.6x token reduction. The Scene-Aware Ranker (SAR) applies 19-class entity recognition and explicit scenario annotations; negative knowledge alone contributes 25 percentage points of Hit@10 gain. On two 100-question benchmarks, Hit@10 rises from 19.1% to 96.6% (+77.5pp) and knowledge coverage from 56% to 77%, at 4.84--5.33s end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [301] [Hierarchical Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27760)
*Peiyu Hu,Siying Gu,Weihai Lu,Zhuodong Liu,Yuntian Tang,Jiahao Liang,Yiying Xie,Jiang Rong,Zhaokai Luo,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HiLaR框架，通过分层潜在推理和层感知强化优化，解决LLM推荐中显式推理开销大及现有潜在推理方法角色不明确的问题，显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐方法中，显式自然语言推理计算开销巨大；而现有的潜在推理方法主要关注中间状态的生成或验证，缺乏对逐层偏好角色及其贡献的充分表征。

Method: 提出HiLaR（Hierarchical Latent Reasoning）框架：1. 构建时间引导的分层用户偏好表示；2. 将其与多个LLM潜在推理状态对齐，实现从广泛偏好到细粒度当前意图的组织；3. 结合最终推荐反馈与各状态边际目标似然增益衍生的层感知过程奖励进行轨迹优化。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上的实验表明，HiLaR普遍优于强大的序列、生成式及基于LLM的推荐基线模型；消融实验验证了分层表示学习、潜在对齐及过程级优化的有效性。

Conclusion: HiLaR通过分层潜在推理机制有效平衡了推理效率与精度，为LLM-based推荐提供了更优的偏好建模与推理优化方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for recommendation by leveraging their semantic understanding and contextual modeling capabilities. Recent studies further introduce reasoning mechanisms to improve user preference modeling. However, explicit natural-language reasoning incurs substantial inference overhead, whereas existing latent reasoning methods mainly focus on generating or verifying intermediate states, leaving their layer-wise preference roles and contributions insufficiently characterized. We propose HiLaR, a Hierarchical Latent Reasoning framework with layer-aware reinforcement optimization for LLM-based recommendation. HiLaR constructs temporal-guided hierarchical user preference representations, aligns them with multiple LLM latent reasoning states, and organizes the reasoning process from broad preferences to fine-grained current intents. To further optimize the reasoning trajectory, HiLaR combines final recommendation feedback with layer-aware process rewards derived from the marginal target-likelihood gain of each state. Experiments on four Amazon benchmark datasets show that HiLaR generally outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-based recommendation baselines. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further verify the contribution of hierarchical representation learning, latent alignment, and process-level optimization. Our code is available in https://github.com/hupeiyu21/HiLaR.

</details>


### [302] [From Understanding to Action: Feedback-Grounded Policy Discovery for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27789)
*Zhi Chen,Minmao Wang,Xingchen Liu,Haoqiang Liang,Huihuang Lin,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Yulong Wang,Shijie Yi,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种反馈驱动的代理框架，通过区分意图知识和策略知识来弥合大语言模型（LLM）在推荐系统中的“理解-行动差距”。该框架利用结果反馈优化推荐策略，并通过双空间关系蒸馏将知识迁移到轻量级语义ID生成器中，实现了无需LLM的高效在线推理，并在A/B测试中显著提升了收益和广告价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语义ID的生成式推荐器主要捕捉行为共现和局部转移，缺乏对用户当前需求的深层理解。虽然大语言模型（LLMs）能通过推理异构交互历史来理解用户需求，但其语言学上合理的推理并不必然转化为有效的推荐决策，存在“理解-行动差距”（Understanding-Action Gap）。此外，LLMs缺乏针对推荐结果的特定反馈训练。

Method: 1. **概念区分**：明确区分“意图知识”（捕捉用户当前需求）和“策略知识”（指定在该需求下的推荐方向和拒绝边界）。
2. **反馈驱动代理框架**：首先诱导任务导向的意图，然后根据相对于仅基于意图基线的增量效用发现推荐策略。
3. **基于结果的评估与优化**：使用基于结果的反馈（而非语言学合理性）对候选策略进行评估和细化。
4. **知识蒸馏**：通过双空间关系蒸馏，将得到的意图和策略知识迁移到轻量级语义ID生成器的两个潜在令牌中，从而实现无LLM的在线推理。

Result: 在公开基准测试中，该方法相比基线模型表现出一致的改进。大规模在线A/B测试显示，收益（Revenue）提升了4.506%，广告价值（ADVV）提升了4.621%。

Conclusion: 通过引入反馈驱动机制并弥合理解与行动之间的差距，该方法有效结合了LLM的推理能力和传统推荐系统的效率，显著提升了推荐效果和业务指标。

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommenders enable efficient next-item generation, but their item-level supervision mainly captures behavioral co-occurrence and local transitions. Large language models (LLMs) can complement these models by reasoning over heterogeneous interaction histories to understand the user's current demand. However, LLMs are not inherently trained with recommendation-specific outcome feedback, and linguistically plausible reasoning therefore does not necessarily lead to effective recommendation decisions. We term this mismatch the Understanding-Action Gap. Accordingly, we distinguish intent knowledge, which captures the user's current demand, from policy knowledge, which specifies the recommendation direction and rejection boundary under that demand. To bridge this gap, we propose a feedback-driven agent framework that first induces task-oriented intent and then discovers recommendation policies according to their incremental utility over an intent-only baseline. Candidate policies are evaluated and refined using outcome-derived feedback rather than linguistic plausibility. We further transfer the resulting intent and policy knowledge into two latent tokens of a lightweight Semantic-ID generator through dual-space relational distillation, enabling LLM-free online inference. Experiments on public benchmarks show consistent improvements over baselines, while large-scale online A/B tests achieve gains of 4.506% in Revenue and 4.621% in ADVV.

</details>


### [303] [Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27944)
*Long Zhang,Hao Jiang,Sheng Yu,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LGRID框架，通过LLM驱动的生成式解耦范式解决现有语义ID（SID）推荐中的语义纠缠和不可解释问题。该框架包含编码、解耦、对齐和量化四个步骤，生成具有明确属性对应关系的可解释SID，在Kuaishou和Foursquare数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义ID（SID）的推荐系统遵循“单一表示后量化”范式，存在两大瓶颈：一是语义纠缠导致地理、品牌、类别等异构属性混合，造成量化过程中的信息丢失、低质量SID及严重碰撞；二是黑盒表征学习缺乏显式的属性语义，使得SID位置缺乏明确的地理或语义含义，削弱了检索可靠性和对SID生成的诊断与控制能力。

Method: 提出LGRID（Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation）框架，采用“编码->解耦->对齐->量化”流水线：1）使用联合LLM编码保留跨属性的地理-语义依赖关系；2）通过结构化解耦块将隐藏状态路由到针对地理和语义因子的属性对齐槽位；3）利用协同对齐学习使这些槽位既具生成可解码性又具检索判别力；4）通过双流残差量化将两个流单独离散化为具有显式属性对应关系的紧凑SID。

Result: 在Kuaishou和Foursquare数据集上的实验表明，LGRID一致性地优于强SID基线模型，相对AUC提升高达5.44%。同时，该方法实现了超过99%的粗粒度地理字段属性解码准确率，并将完整SID的碰撞率从LGSID的97.0%降低至39.9%，证明了其生成SID的可解释性和有效性。

Conclusion: LGRID通过引入生成式解耦范式，成功解决了SID生成中的语义纠缠和不可解释性问题。其设计的结构化解耦块和双流残差量化机制能够分别离散化地理和语义因素，生成位置 grounded 于物品属性和本地服务语义的可解释SID，从而提升了检索性能和系统的可控性。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced ID-based recommendation through Semantic ID (SID) modeling, existing SID generation frameworks largely follow a single-representation-then-quantization paradigm. This design faces two bottlenecks: semantic entanglement mixes heterogeneous attributes, such as geography, brand, and category, causing information loss during quantization, low-quality SIDs, and severe collisions; moreover, black-box representation learning provides neither explicit attribute semantics nor clear geographic or semantic meanings for SID positions. These limitations weaken both retrieval reliability and the ability to diagnose or control SID generation. We propose Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation (LGRID). LGRID introduces a generative disentanglement paradigm through an Encode -> Disentangle -> Align -> Quantize pipeline. It first uses joint LLM encoding to preserve cross-attribute geographic-semantic dependencies, rather than encoding fields independently. A Structured Disentangled Block then routes hidden states into attribute-aligned slots for geographic and semantic factors. Synergistic Alignment Learning makes these slots both generatively decodable and discriminative for retrieval, while Dual-Stream Residual Quantization separately discretizes the two streams into compact SIDs with explicit attribute correspondence. This design yields interpretable SIDs with positions grounded in item attributes and local-service semantics. Experiments on Kuaishou and Foursquare show that LGRID consistently outperforms strong SID baselines, achieving up to a 5.44 percent relative AUC gain. It also achieves over 99 percent attribute-decoding accuracy for coarse geographic fields and reduces the full-SID collision rate to 39.9 percent, compared with 97.0 percent for LGSID.

</details>


### [304] [VIG-RL: Learning to Search and Insert for Verified Image Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28055)
*Qinhan Yu,Jun Guang,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出VIG-RL框架，通过强化学习在动态ReAct循环中自主决策检索、选择和插入视觉证据，解决知识密集型场景下图文响应可靠性问题，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强框架依赖解耦的静态流水线，无法动态推理何时需要外部知识以及在哪里上下文插入视觉资产，导致在知识密集型场景中难以提供可靠的交错文本-图像响应。

Method: 提出VIG-RL自主智能体框架，将搜索-选择-插入工作流构建为主动决策过程；在动态ReAct风格循环中运行，并通过包含逐步工具执行和最终多模态对齐的复合奖励系统进行强化学习优化。

Result: 广泛评估表明，VIG-RL建立了新的最先进（SOTA）水平，显著优于现有的静态基线方法。

Conclusion: VIG-RL通过动态决策和强化学习优化，有效解决了Verified Image Grounding中的关键挑战，提升了多模态响应的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: In knowledge-intensive scenarios, providing reliable interleaved text-image responses requires Verified Image Grounding (VIG): the precise integration of retrieved authentic visual evidence. Existing retrieval-augmented frameworks predominantly rely on decoupled, static pipelines, inherently failing to dynamically reason about when external knowledge is required and where visual assets should be contextually inserted. To bridge this gap, we propose VIG-RL, an autonomous agentic framework that formulates the search-selection-insertion workflow as an active decision-making process. Operating within a dynamic ReAct-style loop, VIG-RL is optimized via reinforcement learning, guided by a composite reward system that holistically evaluates the agent's step-by-step tool execution and final multimodal alignment. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VIG-RL establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming existing static baselines.

</details>


### [305] [CCFormer: Efficient Cross-Field Interaction and Hierarchical Sequence Compression for Industrial Recommendation at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28070)
*Yunlong Wang,Huizhe Zhang,Haonan Hu,Yudong Li,Bing Wen,Jianchao Tu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CCFormer，一种高效的Transformer骨干网络，通过特征-域分离交叉注意力和长序列子空间token混合，解决工业推荐系统中延迟/资源约束与细粒度特征交互、长序列建模之间的平衡难题。在腾讯两个场景上线，提升CTR 3.57%和广告收入1.71%，训练加速2.21倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自注意力的序列推荐模型虽受益于可扩展性定律，但实际工业推荐系统面临严格的延迟和资源限制，难以在计算开销与细粒度特征交互之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出CCFormer，结合特征-域分离的交叉注意力机制以利用异构特征域的长期偏好信号，以及长序列子空间token混合；采用具有渐进扩大感受野的分层序列压缩策略，实现高效长序列建模并减少信息损失。

Result: 在两个公共基准和一个大规模工业数据集上显著优于最先进基线。腾讯视频推荐场景CTR提升3.57%，广告排序场景广告收入提升1.71%，训练速度比HSTU基线快2.21倍。

Conclusion: CCFormer兼具高性能与高效率，已全量部署于腾讯生产环境服务主流量，验证了其在工业推荐系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Recent studies in industrial recommendation systems have demonstrated that sequential recommendation models built upon self-attention can benefit from predictable scaling laws by increasing sequence length and model capacity. However, practical recommender systems impose strict latency and resource constraints, making it challenging to balance computational overhead with fine-grained feature interaction. In this paper, we propose CCFormer, an efficient Transformer backbone that unifies cross-field feature interaction and compressed long-sequence modeling for industrial recommendation. Specifically, CCFormer combines feature-field separated cross attention with long-sequence subspace token mixing to exploit long-term preference signals across heterogeneous feature domains. A hierarchical sequence compression strategy with progressively expanded receptive fields enables efficient long-sequence modeling with reduced information loss. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that CCFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Online A/B tests in a video recommendation scenario and an advertising ranking scenario at Tencent further validate its industrial practicality, yielding a 3.57% CTR gain and a 1.71% advertising revenue lift, respectively, while accelerating model training by 2.21x over the strong HSTU baseline. CCFormer has been fully deployed in Tencent's production recommendation system, serving the main traffic of both scenarios.

</details>


### [306] [TCA-SIR: Learning Target-Conditioned Abstractions for Scientific Inspiration Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28498)
*Yuto Suzuki,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TCA-SIR方法，将科学灵感检索重构为目标条件抽象（TCA），通过提取可迁移的抽象原则而非仅依赖主题相似度，显著提升了远程灵感的检索效果及可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学灵感检索（SIR）方法主要基于主题相似性排序，无法显式表达候选灵感如何迁移到目标问题，特别是在处理依赖“可复用问题解决原理”而非“主题重叠”的远程灵感时存在局限。

Method: 将SIR重构为目标条件抽象（TCA）。模型学习生成针对特定目标的抽象原则，并利用这些表示来预测可迁移性。具体实现为TCA-SIR系统。

Result: 在ResearchBench数据集上，TCA-SIR优于先前的SIR方法和直接的大语言模型检索；相比MOOSE-Chem，HitRate@top4提升了超过10个百分点。此外，学到的抽象比未训练的提示词更清晰地恢复了与目标相关的机制。

Conclusion: TCA-SIR不仅实现了更强的检索性能，还提供了可解释的科学灵感推理依据，证明了从源问题中提取并重新映射可迁移方面对于AI辅助科学假设生成的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific hypothesis generation for AI for Science typically involves Scientific Inspiration Retrieval (SIR) followed by hypothesis composition. Existing SIR methods rank papers by topical similarity and do not explicitly represent how a candidate inspiration transfers to a target problem. This is especially limiting for remote inspirations, whose value often lies in reusable problem-solving principles rather than topical overlap. Motivated by how humans abstract transferable aspects of a source and remap them to a new target, we reformulate SIR as target-conditioned abstraction (TCA). The retrieval object is a transferable abstract principle extracted from a candidate specifically for the target. We present TCA-SIR, which learns to generate target-conditioned abstractions and uses their representations to predict transferability. On ResearchBench, TCA-SIR outperforms prior SIR methods and direct LLM retrieval, improving HitRate@top4% over MOOSE-Chem by more than 10 percentage points. Learned abstractions also recover target-relevant mechanisms more clearly than an untrained TCA prompt, yielding both stronger retrieval and an interpretable rationale for scientific inspiration.

</details>


### [307] [MagicSelector: Joint Optimization for Agent Tool Selection via Counterfactual Decomposition and Progressive Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17751)
*HONOR Agentic Search Team,Zhengzong Chen,Lei Tang,Lijun Liu,Chuandi Jiang,Fan Yang,Keyun Chu,Chu Zhao,Shihao Liu,Minghang Li,Bo Liang,Can Wen,Hailong Wu,Jingnan Ju,Mian Liu,Nengbin Zhang,Peiqiang Wang,Penghe Nie,Qinhui Gu,Sijia Lv,Siqi Chen,Wei Zhang,Yang Xu,Yuhao Qian,Yuxiang Zhang,Zeng Cheng,Zhen Wang,Zuan Chen,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MagicSelector框架，通过反事实任务分解、渐进式重排和动态Top-K策略，解决智能体工具检索中的噪声干扰和上下文分散问题，显著提升检索精度与OOD泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体在工具检索中面临的根本挑战，特别是模糊用户指令导致的冗余噪声和域外（OOD）场景下的严重上下文分散问题。

Method: 构建包含三大核心组件的联合优化框架：(1) 偏好引导的反事实任务分解机制，利用反事实奖励量化分解对检索排序的边际因果增益；(2) 基于自蒸馏难负样本挖掘的渐进式工具重排方法，优化点级和列表级相关性；(3) 双语义边界感知的动态Top-K策略，自适应监控重排分数悬崖和语义偏移以截断候选列表。

Result: 在构建的MTDTool基准（面向移动多轮交互的任务分解基准）上进行了评估，实验表明MagicSelector在工具检索准确率、OOD泛化能力和整体Token效率方面显著优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效提升了智能体工具检索的性能，证明了该联合优化方法在处理复杂指令和减少噪声方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present MagicSelector, a joint optimization framework integrating Counterfactual task decomposition, Progressive reranking, and Dynamic Top-K, designed to address the fundamental challenges of tool retrieval in agents. MagicSelector is a specialized framework capable of translating ambiguous user instructions into executable atomic subtasks and guiding high-precision tool retrieval, effectively mitigating redundant noise and severe context distraction in out-of-domain (OOD) scenarios. We empower MagicSelector with these capabilities through three key contributions: (1) a preference-guided counterfactual task decomposition mechanism that utilizes a counterfactual reward to quantify the marginal causal gain of decomposition on retrieval ranking, effectively imposing fine-grained structural supervision on logical coherence; (2) a progressive tool reranking method driven by self-distillation hard negative mining, which optimizes both point-wise and list-wise relevance to enhance fine-grained discrimination among highly similar tools; and (3) a dual semantic boundary-aware dynamic Top-K strategy that adaptively monitors reranking score cliffs and inter-tool semantic shifts to dynamically truncate the candidate list, maximizing relevant tool recall while filtering long-tail noise. Evaluated on MTDTool, the first task decomposition benchmark we constructed tailored for mobile multi-turn interactions with process-level annotations, MagicSelector yields promising performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MagicSelector significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of tool retrieval accuracy, OOD generalization capability, and overall token efficiency, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [308] [AgenticER: the next frontier in Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27435)
*George Papadakis,Panos Korovesis,Manolis Koubarakis,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将实体解析（ER）从传统的“被动范式”转变为“智能体驱动（Agentic ER）”的新范式，通过自主智能体进行迭代式证据收集、推理和决策，以解决数据湖中异构流数据的模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ER方法多为基于静态相似度的“被动范式”，无法应对数据湖中异构格式（如CSV、JSON、RDF及自由文本）带来的不确定性和上下文依赖性，难以处理需要多源推理和人工介入的复杂场景。

Method: 将ER形式化为决策理论问题，提出Agentic ER参考架构，利用自主智能体主动规划策略、获取外部证据、决定查询时机，并优化准确性、成本与延迟之间的权衡。

Result: 明确了Agentic ER的核心研究挑战，提出了针对智能体行为的新评估维度，确立了数据管理与智能体交叉领域的新研究方向。

Conclusion: Agentic ER通过引入自主决策和迭代机制，有效弥补了传统被动方法在处理动态、异构数据时的不足，为未来ER研究提供了新的理论框架和实践路径。

Abstract: Entity Resolution (ER) is a fundamental problem in data management, playing a critical role in tasks like data cleaning and knowledge graph construction. The existing ER approaches range from traditional rule-based to deep learning techniques and LLM-based methods, but typically operate under a ``passive paradigm'', as duplicates are detected through static, one-shot similarity computations. Such approaches fail to capture the inherently uncertain and context-dependent nature of real-world ER tasks, especially in data lakes with streaming content in heterogeneous formats such as CSV files, JSON files, RDF dumps, and free text. In such settings, resolving ambiguity often requires iterative evidence gathering, reasoning across multiple sources, even selective human involvement. To cover this gap, we advocate a paradigm shift from passive to Agentic ER, which frames ER as a sequential decision-making process that is performed by autonomous agents. These agents actively plan ER strategies, acquire external evidence, decide when to query additional sources or humans, and optimize trade-offs between accuracy, cost, and latency. We formalize Agentic ER as a decision-theoretic problem, we propose a reference architecture, we identify core research challenges, and outline new evaluation dimensions tailored to agentic behavior. By introducing Agentic ER, we aim to establish a new research direction at the intersection of data management and intelligent agents.

</details>


### [309] [Fully Inductive Cardinality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28311)
*Tim Schwabe,Lukas Ketzer,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出FICE，首个能在未见知识图谱上泛化且无需重训练的完全归纳式BGP查询基数估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习基数估计器因具有归纳偏置（transductive），在图结构变化或应用于新图时需重新训练，阻碍了其在真实世界三元组存储中的部署。

Method: 构建包含编码器GNN和解码器GNN的联合模型：编码器基于KG因子图视图生成实体和关系嵌入（利用2-hop邻域局部性证明）；解码器沿查询连接拓扑组合嵌入以预测对数基数。采用邻域采样扩展至百万级三元组规模，并解耦嵌入生成与基数解码以降低延迟。

Result: 在10个知识图谱上的实验表明，FICE将整体中位数q-error从最佳竞争者的13.54降低至5.34，并在尾部行为表现上优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: FICE实现了跨未见图谱的零样本泛化能力，显著提升了基数估计精度与效率，解决了现有学习方法缺乏泛化性的问题。

Abstract: Query optimization of Basic Graph Patterns (BGP) SPARQL queries over Knowledge Graphs (KG) requires accurate cardinality estimation. Recently published learned estimators outperform statistics- and sampling-based approaches, but share a limitation preventing their adoption in real-world triplestores: they are transductive and require retraining when the underlying graph changes or when applied to new graphs. We present FICE (Fully Inductive Cardinality Estimation), the first learned cardinality estimator for BGP queries over KGs that generalizes to entirely unseen graphs (including unseen relations), without any retraining. FICE is a graph neural network (GNN) with two coupled components. First, an encoder GNN over a factor-graph view of the KG produces entity and relation embeddings. We prove that BGP cardinality is a local function of the 2-hop neighborhood around bound terms in this view, motivating the local message-passing encoder. A decoder GNN then composes these embeddings along the join topology of the query to predict log-cardinality. The encoder and decoder are trained jointly, making the embeddings specialized for cardinality estimation. FICE is trained using neighborhood sampling to scale to KGs with millions of triples, and decouples embedding generation from cardinality decoding to enable estimation latency below a millisecond. Compared to learned and non-learned baselines over 10 KGs, FICE reduces the overall median q-error from 13.54 (for the best competitor) to 5.34 and dominates all approaches in tail behavior.

</details>
